await (async page=>{
 const out='/home/vlad_/projects/CyberAlchemy-lab/output/deployments/cyberalchemize-diagrams-20261015',base='https://cyberalchemize.com/';const rows=[],failures=[],errors=[],audits=[],checks=[];page.on('pageerror',e=>errors.push(String(e)));const cdp=await page.context().newCDPSession(page);await cdp.send('Network.setCacheDisabled',{cacheDisabled:true});await page.emulateMedia({reducedMotion:'reduce'});
 const record=(kind,data)=>{rows.push({kind,viewport:page.viewportSize(),...data});if(data.failures?.length)failures.push({kind,...data})};
 const ready=async()=>{await page.evaluate(async()=>{await document.fonts.ready;await Promise.all([...document.images].filter(i=>i.getAttribute('src')).map(async i=>{i.loading='eager';try{await i.decode()}catch{}}))});};
 const geometry=async(selector)=>page.locator(selector).evaluate(e=>{const r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return{width:r.width,height:r.height,scrollX:e.scrollWidth>e.clientWidth+1,scrollY:e.scrollHeight>e.clientHeight+1,hidden:!r.width,pageOverflow:document.documentElement.scrollWidth>innerWidth+1}});
 const shot=async(selector,name)=>{await page.locator(selector).evaluate(e=>window.scrollTo(0,e.getBoundingClientRect().top+scrollY-110));await page.screenshot({path:out+'/'+name+'.png'});};
 for(const width of [320,390,768,1440,1920])for(const mode of ['light','dark']){
  await page.setViewportSize({width,height:width===320?700:width===390?844:900});const query='?mode='+mode+'&review=diagrams-v1-'+width;
  await page.goto(base+'governance-lab-series/governance-lab-reference.html'+query+'#system-map');await ready();await page.locator('.smap-visual').waitFor({state:'visible'});
  const initial=await page.locator('#map-guide-mode').getAttribute('aria-pressed');const mapWidth=await page.locator('#system-map').evaluate(e=>e.clientWidth);checks.push({name:'readable map default '+width+' '+mode,mapWidth,pass:initial===(mapWidth<960?'true':'false')});
  const views=await page.locator('[data-map-view]').evaluateAll(es=>es.map(e=>e.dataset.mapView));
  for(const view of views){await page.locator('[data-map-view="'+view+'"]').click();const lenses=view==='governance'?await page.locator('[data-governance-focus]').evaluateAll(es=>es.map(e=>e.dataset.governanceFocus)):[null];
   for(const lens of lenses){if(lens)await page.locator('[data-governance-focus="'+lens+'"]').click();await page.locator('#map-diagram-mode').click();const full=await geometry('.smap-stage');record('map whole',{width,mode,view,lens,...full,failures:[full.scrollX&&'horizontal scroll',full.scrollY&&'vertical scroll',full.height>page.viewportSize().height-120&&'taller than view',full.pageOverflow&&'page overflow',full.hidden&&'hidden'].filter(Boolean)});
    await page.locator('#map-guide-mode').click();const model=await page.evaluate(()=>JSON.parse(document.getElementById('system-map-data').textContent));const v=model.views.find(v=>v.id===view),graph=lens?v.focus_views.find(f=>f.focus_key===lens):v;
    const guided=await page.evaluate(({edges})=>{const states=[];for(let i=0;i<edges.length;i++){if(i)document.getElementById('map-guide-next').click();const root=document.getElementById('map-guided-reader'),e=edges[i],r=root.getBoundingClientRect();states.push({index:i,source:document.getElementById('map-guide-source').querySelector('strong').textContent,target:document.getElementById('map-guide-target').querySelector('strong').textContent,label:document.getElementById('map-guide-label').textContent,copy:document.getElementById('map-guide-copy').textContent,height:r.height,scrollX:root.scrollWidth>root.clientWidth+1,scrollY:root.scrollHeight>root.clientHeight+1});}return states;},{edges:graph.edges});
    for(const [i,state]of guided.entries()){const edge=graph.edges[i];record('map connection',{width,mode,view,lens,...state,failures:[state.source!==model.nodes[edge.source].title&&'source mismatch',state.target!==model.nodes[edge.target].title&&'target mismatch',state.label!==edge.label&&'label mismatch',state.copy!==edge.explanation&&'explanation mismatch',state.scrollX&&'horizontal scroll',state.height>page.viewportSize().height-120&&'taller than view'].filter(Boolean)});}
    if(view==='overview'&&[390,1440].includes(width)){await shot('#map-guided-reader','map-guide-'+width+'-'+mode);await page.locator('#map-diagram-mode').click();await shot('.smap-stage','map-whole-'+width+'-'+mode);await page.locator('#map-guide-mode').click();}
   }
  }
  const labs=['evidence-chain','admission-and-histories','meaning-shape-record','records-and-ontology','agent-session','purpose-to-implementation','learning-loop'];
  for(const [kind,ids]of [['lab',labs],['craft',Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1))]]){
   for(const id of ids){const name=kind==='lab'?'governance-lab-series/governance-lab-reference.html':'craft-documentation-proposal/craft-model.html';await page.goto(base+name+query+'#figure-'+id);const figure=page.locator('#figure-'+id);await figure.waitFor({state:'visible'});await ready();const reader=figure.locator('[data-diagram-reading]');await reader.locator('[data-diagram-mode=guide]').click();
    const states=await reader.evaluate(root=>{const steps=JSON.parse(root.querySelector('.diagram-reading-data').textContent),states=[];for(let i=0;i<steps.length;i++){if(i)root.querySelector('[data-diagram-next]').click();const panel=root.querySelector('.diagram-reader'),r=panel.getBoundingClientRect();states.push({index:i,title:root.querySelector('[data-diagram-step-title]').textContent,copy:root.querySelector('[data-diagram-step-copy]').textContent,height:r.height,scrollX:panel.scrollWidth>panel.clientWidth+1,scrollY:panel.scrollHeight>panel.clientHeight+1,match:root.querySelector('[data-diagram-step-title]').textContent===steps[i][0]&&root.querySelector('[data-diagram-step-copy]').textContent===steps[i][1]});}return states;});
    for(const state of states)record('figure step',{width,mode,figure:kind+'-'+id,...state,failures:[!state.match&&'copy mismatch',state.scrollX&&'horizontal scroll',state.height>page.viewportSize().height-120&&'taller than view'].filter(Boolean)});
    if((kind==='lab'&&id==='agent-session')||(kind==='craft'&&id==='3'))if([390,1440].includes(width))await shot('#figure-'+id,'figure-'+kind+'-'+width+'-'+mode);
    await reader.locator('[data-diagram-mode=whole]').click();const full=await reader.locator('.diagram-reading-full').evaluate(e=>{const r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return{width:r.width,height:r.height,scrollX:e.scrollWidth>e.clientWidth+1,scrollY:e.scrollHeight>e.clientHeight+1,broken:[...e.querySelectorAll('img')].some(i=>!i.complete||!i.naturalWidth),minWidth:getComputedStyle(e.querySelector('svg,img')).minWidth}});record('figure whole',{width,mode,figure:kind+'-'+id,...full,failures:[full.width===0&&'hidden',full.scrollX&&'horizontal scroll',full.broken&&'broken image',full.height>page.viewportSize().height-100&&'taller than view'].filter(Boolean)});
   }
  }
  for(const [name,selector]of [['company-brain.html','.brain-map-field'],['index.html','.system-flow'],['hackathon.html','.hack-sketch']]){await page.goto(base+'governance-lab-series/'+name+query);await ready();const g=await geometry(selector);record('role or hero diagram',{width,mode,name,...g,failures:[g.pageOverflow&&'page overflow',g.scrollX&&'horizontal scroll',g.height>page.viewportSize().height-120&&'taller than view'].filter(Boolean)});if([390,1440].includes(width))await shot(selector,'role-'+name.replace('.html','')+'-'+width+'-'+mode);}
 }
 // Keyboard and selection stay in the reader; pagination does not move the page.
 await page.setViewportSize({width:390,height:900});await page.goto(base+'governance-lab-series/governance-lab-reference.html?mode=light#system-map');await page.locator('#map-guide-mode').click();await page.locator('#map-guide-next').focus();const before=await page.evaluate(()=>scrollY);await page.keyboard.press('Enter');const after=await page.evaluate(()=>scrollY);checks.push({name:'keyboard advances connection without page scroll',pass:before===after&&(await page.locator('#map-guide-position').innerText()).startsWith('2 /')});await page.locator('#map-guide-source').click();checks.push({name:'guided concept opens the existing inspector',pass:(await page.locator('#map-node-title').innerText())===(await page.locator('#map-guide-source strong').innerText())});
 for(const name of ['governance-lab-series/governance-lab-reference.html?mode=light#system-map','governance-lab-series/governance-lab-reference.html?mode=dark#evidence','craft-documentation-proposal/craft-model.html?mode=light#figure-3','craft-documentation-proposal/craft-model.html?mode=dark#figure-7']){await page.goto(base+name);await ready();await page.addScriptTag({path:'/home/vlad_/.npm/_npx/0f94ee7615faf582/node_modules/axe-core/axe.min.js'});const a=await page.evaluate(async()=>{const r=await axe.run(document,{runOnly:{type:'tag',values:['wcag2a','wcag2aa','wcag21aa','wcag22aa','best-practice']}});return r.violations});audits.push({name,violations:a});}
 await page.goto(base+'craft-documentation-proposal/craft-model.html?mode=light#figure-3');await page.locator('#figure-3 [data-diagram-mode=guide]').click();await page.locator('#figure-3 [data-diagram-next]').focus();await page.keyboard.press('Enter');checks.push({name:'keyboard advances static figure',pass:(await page.locator('#figure-3 [data-diagram-position]').innerText())==='2 / 3'});await page.locator('#figure-3 .diagram-open').click();await page.locator('#diagram-dialog').waitFor({state:'visible'});await page.locator('#dialog-image').evaluate(i=>i.decode());checks.push({name:'full-size Craft dialog also fits without panning',pass:await page.locator('.dialog-image-wrap').evaluate(e=>e.scrollWidth<=e.clientWidth+1&&e.scrollHeight<=e.clientHeight+1)});await page.keyboard.press('Escape');checks.push({name:'diagram dialog closes with Escape',pass:!(await page.locator('#diagram-dialog').isVisible())});
 const context=await page.context().browser().newContext({javaScriptEnabled:false,viewport:{width:390,height:900}});try{const p=await context.newPage();await p.goto(base+'governance-lab-series/governance-lab-reference.html');const g=await p.locator('.smap-stage').evaluate(e=>({w:e.clientWidth,s:e.scrollWidth,h:e.clientHeight,sh:e.scrollHeight}));checks.push({name:'no-JS full map fits',pass:g.w>0&&g.s<=g.w+1&&g.sh<=g.h+1});await p.goto(base+'craft-documentation-proposal/craft-model.html');const f=await p.locator('.diagram-scroll').evaluateAll(es=>es.every(e=>e.scrollWidth<=e.clientWidth+1));checks.push({name:'no-JS Craft diagrams fit',pass:f});}finally{await context.close()}
 return{summary:{diagramStates:rows.length,failures:failures.length,runtimeErrors:errors.length,a11yStates:audits.length,a11yViolations:audits.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.violations.length,0),interactionChecks:checks.length,interactionFailures:checks.filter(c=>!c.pass).length},rows,failures,errors,audits,checks};
}
)(page);
